```css
/* The Sponsor Dashboard's base styles: enough shape to read without the theme; the theme's
   skin (wpcredits-theme 1.18.0) dresses everything under .wpc-sponsor-page. */
.wpcpm-sponsor__identity { display: flex; gap: 24px; align-items: center; margin: 0 0 24px; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__logo { flex: 0 0 176px; width: 176px; height: 96px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; background: #fff; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 8px; overflow: hidden; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__logo-image { max-width: 160px; max-height: 80px; width: auto; height: auto; object-fit: contain; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__initials { font-size: 32px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 0.04em; color: #1e1e1e; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__name { margin: 0 0 4px; font-size: 24px; font-weight: 700; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__fact, .wpcpm-sponsor__website { margin: 0 0 2px; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__card { margin: 0 0 16px; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__form .wpcpm-sponsor__field { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px; margin: 0 0 12px; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__form input[type="text"], .wpcpm-sponsor__form input[type="url"], .wpcpm-sponsor__form input[type="email"], .wpcpm-sponsor__form select, .wpcpm-sponsor__form textarea { max-width: 640px; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__choices { border: 0; padding: 0; margin: 0 0 12px; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__choices label { display: block; margin: 0 0 6px; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__subheading { margin: 20px 0 8px; font-size: 16px; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__mentors, .wpcpm-sponsor__looking, .wpcpm-sponsor__people, .wpcpm-sponsor__history { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__mentors li, .wpcpm-sponsor__looking li, .wpcpm-sponsor__people li, .wpcpm-sponsor__history li { padding: 8px 0; border-top: 1px solid #eee; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__counts, .wpcpm-sponsor__expertise, .wpcpm-sponsor__muted { color: #555; font-size: 14px; }
.wpcpm-sponsor__looking .wpcpm-inline-form { display: inline-block; margin-left: 8px; }
.wpcpm-dashboard__message { padding: 12px 16px; border-left: 4px solid #999; margin: 0 0 16px; }
.wpcpm-dashboard__message--success { border-left-color: #1a7f37; }
.wpcpm-dashboard__message--error { border-left-color: #b32d2e; }
.wpcpm-dashboard__message--info { border-left-color: #2271b1; }
```

```css
/*
 * The Sponsor Dashboard (theme 1.18.0). The same two answers as the institution and
 * administrator pages, because its groups sit inside a section already inset by 32px: the
 * clickable row loses its horizontal margin and the open body its, or every row steps 32px
 * right of the identity block above it (theme 1.16.5 and 1.16.6 were this bug).
 */
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-group__summary,
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-group__disclosure[open] .wpcpm-group__body {
	margin-left: 0;
	margin-right: 0;
}

.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__identity,
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__card {
	padding-left: 32px;
	padding-right: 32px;
}

/*
 * The logo box: 176px wide, the image contained, on a white ground whatever the page's
 * surface, because half the sponsors drew their logo for a dark background and a
 * transparent PNG of white letters vanishes on white and reads on nothing else.
 */
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__logo {
	width: 176px;
	height: 96px;
	background: #fff;
	border: 1px solid var( --wpc-line-card );
	border-radius: var( --wpc-radius-card );
}

.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__logo-image {
	object-fit: contain;
}

.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__initials {
	color: var( --wpc-brand );
	font: 700 32px/1 var( --wpc-font );
}

.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__name {
	font: 700 28px/34px var( --wpc-font );
}

.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__fact,
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__website {
	font: 400 15px/22px var( --wpc-font );
}

/* The program-contact block sits in the resources section's right column, like the institution's. */
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-resources__contact {
	border-top: 1px solid var( --wpc-line-soft );
	padding-top: 16px;
	margin-top: 16px;
}

/* The forms: the theme's field treatment, the theme's buttons. */
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__form input[type="text"],
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__form input[type="url"],
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__form input[type="email"],
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__form select,
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__form textarea {
	border: 1px solid var( --wpc-line );
	border-radius: 8px;
	font: inherit;
	padding: 8px 10px;
	width: 100%;
}

.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__field > label {
	font: 600 14px/20px var( --wpc-font );
}

.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__subheading {
	font: 700 16px/22px var( --wpc-font );
}

.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__mentors li,
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__looking li,
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__people li,
.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-sponsor__history li {
	border-top: 1px solid var( --wpc-line-soft );
}

.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-dashboard__message--success {
	border-left-color: var( --wpc-green );
}

.wpc-sponsor-page .wpcpm-dashboard__message--error {
	border-left-color: var( --wpc-red );
}
```

### Task 12: Docs, versions, checks, zips; then the release

**Files:**
- Modify: `wpcredits-program-manager.php:6,22`, `readme.txt:7` and the changelog, `docs/sections/34-admin-operations.md` (options table rows and a `## Sponsors` section before the section holding the table), `docs/sections/30-admin-wpadmin.md` (one paragraph on the Sponsors screen)

- [ ] **Step 1: Version and changelog**

`Version:` header and `WPCPM_VERSION` to `1.93.0`; `Stable tag: 1.93.0`; above the newest `= 1.92.x =` entry:

```
= 1.93.0 =
* **The Sponsors module, phase one: accounts, sync and the Sponsor Dashboard.** A nightly sync reads the Team Members and Sponsors tables into an index and copies each Approved sponsor's logo into the Media Library (an Airtable attachment URL expires within hours). A program manager creates each sponsor's account one at a time from the Sponsors screen, from the contact address, with the welcome through the invitation queue; accounts are attached and removed there too, and the base's Dashboard account checkbox follows. Sponsors get a Sponsor Dashboard at /sponsor-dashboard/: logo, name, website, product type and contact; their contact at the program; a profile they can save back to Airtable through an allowlist with an audit row; their sponsored mentors with student counts and never a student's name; the mentors looking for a sponsor, with one press to say they would like to sponsor one; and a form to say what else they would like to support, which mails their program contact and appends a dated line to the base's Sponsorship interests column. One policy decides every sponsor action; every refusal of a member is metered, twenty a day.
* Extracted for both modules: `WPCPM_Refusal_Meter` (the institution roster's twenty-a-day lock) and `WPCPM_Image_Upload` (the one image handler: PNG, JPEG or WebP, re-saved through WordPress's editor, never SVG). The audit log gains a sponsor key. The welcome mail, the settings and the audience maps know sponsors. The mentors sync keeps a sponsorship index of every Active mentor.
* Five new Airtable columns on the Sponsors table: Agreement Status, Agreement Accepted On, Agreement Document, Sponsorship interests, Dashboard account.
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The administrators guide**

`docs/sections/34-admin-operations.md`: the options table gains, in alphabetical position, rows for `wpcpm_mentors_sponsorship` (`WPCPM_Mentors_Sync::OPT_SPONSORSHIP`), `wpcpm_sponsor_logo_<record>` (`WPCPM_Sponsors_Index::OPT_LOGO_PREFIX`), `wpcpm_sponsor_page_id`, `wpcpm_sponsor_page_title_fixed`, `wpcpm_sponsors_index`, `wpcpm_sponsors_last_error`, `wpcpm_sponsors_last_sync`, `wpcpm_sponsors_lock`, `wpcpm_sponsors_report`, `wpcpm_sponsors_state`, `wpcpm_team_members`; and a section:

```
## Sponsors

The Sponsors screen is where a sponsor's account begins. The nightly sync (four hours after the institutions sync) reads the Team Members table and the Sponsors table into an index, copies each Approved sponsor's logo into the Media Library, and, only if the setting says so, detaches the accounts of sponsors that are no longer Approved. It never creates an account: a program manager presses Create account on an Approved sponsor's row, which makes an account from the sponsor's Contact Email (or attaches the account that already holds that address), queues the welcome, ticks the Dashboard account checkbox in Airtable and writes a log row. Accounts are attached by address and removed on the same screen; a sponsor's dashboard is opened through the switcher link in the first column. A sponsor whose logo is an SVG is named in the sync report: the site does not take SVG, and the sponsor converts it.

On the Sponsor Dashboard a sponsor can save eight profile fields back to Airtable (website, contact person and email, product type, offer, instructions, more-info link and the free-text field); every save writes an audit row naming the fields changed and nothing else. Interests, and interest in a mentor from the looking-for-a-sponsor list, mail the assigned program manager (the Person of contact in Airtable), or the addresses in the "Interest mail" setting, or every program manager, in that order of preference; five a day per account. What sponsors can never read is a student's name: their mentors card shows mentor names and student counts only.
```

`docs/sections/30-admin-wpadmin.md` gains one paragraph: "Since 1.93.0 the Sponsors screen is no longer a placeholder: it holds the sponsors sync, every sponsor with its status, program contact and accounts, the Create account and Attach account controls, and the log of interests sponsors expressed on their dashboard."
